a final dividend of HK$0.06 per share for the year ended 31 December 2025,        截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度末期股息
payable to shareholders of record as of 30 April 2026. The proposal is subject    每股 0.06 港元予於二零二六年四月三十日名列本

persistent structural challenges, with GDP improvement largely buoyed             結構性挑戰，本地生產總值的改善主要受惠於強

inflation remained modest, shifting consumer behavior continued to weigh          維持溫和，但消費者行為的轉變繼續為本地零售
heavily on domestic retail performance. A key factor was the sustained surge      帶來壓力。其中一個關鍵因素是北上消費的熱潮
in outbound tourism to Mainland China, which reached record levels in             持續，二零二五年出境人次更創下歷史新高；這
2025 as residents were drawn by greater travel convenience, favorable price       主要是隨著跨境交通日益便利、價格優勢顯著，
differentials, and evolving lifestyle preferences. This diverted local spending   以及市民生活方式偏好有所轉變所致。這種消費
across the border, constraining retail growth and highlighting strains in Hong    外流趨勢將本地消費力轉移至內地，限制了零售
Kong’s post-pandemic stabilization.                                               業增長，亦突顯了香港在疫情後穩定過程中所面
                                                                                  臨的壓力。

The Group’s risk management framework is based on the “Three Lines of            本集團風險管理架構採用「三道防線」模式。
Defense” model.

1.     The management team is responsible for the continuous identification,     1.    管理層負責持續鑒定、評估、監察及呈報

       their respective areas; the planning and implementation of actions              行管理風險的行動；及向執行董事及業務
       to manage these risks; and escalation of these risks that exceed the            監控團隊上報超過可容忍限度的風險。
       tolerance limits to the managing director and Business Control team.

2.     The Business Control team conducts periodic review and challenge of       2.    業務監控團隊進行定期審閱及測試對本集
       top risks impacting the Group’s strategic objectives; escalates the top         團策略目標有影響的主要風險；向高級管
       risks to senior management and through them, to the audit committee             理層上報關鍵風險，並經他們上報至審核
       and the board for their review; and facilitates the risk management             委員會及董事會作審閱；及促進風險管理
       process.                                                                        流程。

3.     The Group Internal Audit Function provides assurance over the             3.    集團內部審核功能為管理風險的監控效能
       effectiveness of controls in place to manage risk.                              提供保證。

        through its power over the entity. When assessing whether the Group has                回報的能力時，即控制了該實體。在評估本

        date that control ceases. Intra-group balances and transactions, and any              團內公司之間之結餘、交易以及交易所產生

        group transactions are eliminated in the same way as unrealised gains,                之間之交易而產生之未變現虧損，乃以未變
        but only to the extent that there is no evidence of impairment.                       現盈利相同之形式抵銷。

(g)     Leased assets                                                                    (g)   租賃資產
(i)     As a lessee                                                                      (i)   作為承租人

        At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether the contract is, or             本集團於合約開始時評估合約是否屬於（或
        contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys         包含）租賃。倘合約為換取代價而轉移在一
        the right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in            段時間內控制已識別資產使用之權利，則該
        exchange for consideration. Control is conveyed where the customer                     合約屬於（或包含）租賃。倘客戶既有權指示
        has both the right to direct the use of the identified asset and to obtain             已識別資產之使用又有權從使用已識別資產
        substantially all of the economic benefits from that use.                              獲得絕大部分經濟利益，則控制權已告轉移。

        Where the contract contains lease components and non-lease                             對於包含租賃部分及非租賃部分之合約，本
        components, the Group has elected not to separate non-lease                            集團已選擇不將非租賃部分分開處理，而是
        components and accounts for each lease component and any associated                    就所有租賃將各租賃部分及任何相關之非租
        non-lease components as a single lease component for all leases.                       賃部分作為單一租賃部分入賬。

        At the lease commencement date, the Group recognises a right-of-use                    本集團於租賃開始日期確認使用權資產及租
        asset and a lease liability, except for short-term leases that have a lease            賃負債，惟租期為十二個月或以下之短期租
        term of 12 months or less and leases of low-value assets which, for the                賃或低價值資產（就本集團而言主要為手提
        Group are primarily laptops and office furniture. When the Group enters                電腦及辦公室傢俬）之租賃除外。當本集團
        into a lease in respect of a low-value asset, the Group decides whether                就低價值資產訂立租賃時，本集團會按租賃
        to capitalise the lease on a lease-by-lease basis. The lease payments                  之個別情況決定是否將租賃資本化。與該等
        associated with those leases which are not capitalised are recognised as               並未資本化之租賃相關之租賃付款於租期內
        an expense on a systematic basis over the lease term.                                  有系統地確認為開支。

        Where the lease is capitalised, the lease liability is initially recognised at         在租賃資本化之情況下，租賃負債初步於租

        After initial recognition, the lease liability is measured at amortised cost           債按攤銷成本計量，而利息支出使用實際利
        and interest expense is recognised using the effective interest method.                率法計算。計量租賃負債時不會計入並非視
        Variable lease payments that do not depend on an index or rate are not                 乎指數或利率而定之可變租賃付款，因此有

        contributions from the relevant employees.

        actuary using the projected unit credit method. For ORSO plans, when the                進賬法計算。就職業退休計劃而言，倘計算
        calculation results in a benefit to the Group, the recognised asset is limited          得出對本集團的利益，則已確認資產以可從

        exchange differences are recognised in other comprehensive income and

        On disposal of an operation outside Hong Kong, the cumulative amount                   當出售香港以外業務時，與該業務有關而確
        of the exchange differences relating to that operation is reclassified from            認於盈利或虧損時的累計匯兌差額會從權益
        equity to profit or loss when the profit or loss on disposal is recognised.            改列為盈利或虧損。

        Foreign exchange gains and losses arising from monetary items that                     貨幣項目產生的外幣匯兌損益（實則屬於香
        in substance form part of the net investment in an operation outside                   港以外業務的淨投資的一部份）與相關之稅
        Hong Kong, together with any related tax, are reclassified to equity on                項應於合併時重新分類並由損益撥入股東權
        consolidation.                                                                         益。

(t)     Borrowing costs                                                                 (t)    借貸成本
        Borrowing costs are expensed in the period in which they are incurred.                 借貸成本均在產生的期間列作開支。

2       Key accounting judgements and estimates                                       2     主要會計判斷及估計
        Impairment of non-current assets                                                    非流動資產之減值
        The Group reviews the carrying amounts of non-current assets at each                本集團於各報告日期檢討非流動資產的賬面
        reporting date to determine whether there is objective evidence of                  值，以確定是否存在減值的客觀證據。當減
        impairment. When indicators of impairment is identified, management                 值指標確定後，管理層確定各自資產或賺取
        determine the recoverable amounts of respective assets or cash-                     現金單位的可收回金額。可收回金額為公允
        generating units. The recoverable amount is the greater of its fair value           價值減銷售成本與使用價值兩者的較高者。
        less costs of disposal and value in use. In determining the fair value less         於釐定公允價值減銷售成本時，管理層會參
        costs of disposal, management refer to the observable market prices                 考相類資產的可觀察市場價格。於釐定使用
        for similar assets. In determining the value in use, expected cash flows            價值時，由使用資產或賺取現金單位產生的
        generated by the assets or cash-generating units are discounted to                  預期現金流需貼現至現值。過程中涉及對收
        their present value, which requires significant judgement relating to the           入預測、未來經營成本估計及貼現率的重大
        revenue forecasts, estimations of future operating costs and discount               判斷。本集團運用所有可用資料以確定可收
        rate. The Group uses all readily available information in determining an            回金額的合理概算。當可收回金額低於賬面
        amount that is a reasonable approximation of the recoverable amount.                值時，減值虧損於損益確認。然而，在現金
        When the recoverable amount is lower than the carrying amount, an                   流量估計中所採用假設的任何變動將增加或
        impairment loss is recognised in profit or loss. However, any change in             減少減值撥備虧損，並影響本集團的財務狀
        the assumptions adopted in the cash flow forecasts would increase or                況。非流動資產的性質和賬面值詳情分別於
        decrease the provision for impairment loss and affect the Group’s financial         附註 11 及 12 中披露。
        position. Details of the nature and carrying amounts of non-current assets
        are disclosed in notes 11 and 12.

        The Group identified a shortfall in the actual results against previous              本集團發現實際業績與過往預測出現差異，
        forecasts due to various factors, including but not limited to, a decline in         因多項因素所致，包括但不限於市場競爭激
        demand for its own brewed products in light of fierce market competition             烈導致自身釀製之產品需求下降以及外遊消
        and more outbound travel spending. Indicators of impairment existed for              費增加。香港製造業務之非流動資產存在減
        the non-current assets employed in the manufacturing operations in Hong              值跡象，主要包括生產廠房及機器。
        Kong, comprising primarily the production plant and machineries.

        The Group determined the recoverable amount of the cash-generating                    本集團於二零二四年十二月三十一日釐定該
        unit to which these assets belong (the “Hong Kong CGU”) to be                         等資產所屬現金產生單位（「香港現金產生單
        HK$227,000,000 as at 31 December 2024, which was lower than the                       位」）之可收回金額為 227,000,000 元，該金
        carrying amount. Based on management’s assessment, impairment losses                  額低於賬面值。根據管理層之評估，截至二
        of HK$90,000,000 were recognised on the property, plant and equipment                 零二四年十二月三十一日止年度，香港現金
        employed in the Hong Kong CGU and presented as “impairment losses                     產生單位所用之物業、機器及設備確認減值
        of non-current assets” in the consolidated income statement for the year              虧損 90,000,000 元，並於綜合收益表列示為
        ended 31 December 2024.                                                              「非流動資產減值虧損」。

        The recoverable amount of the Hong Kong CGU was determined based                     香港現金產生單位的可收回金額是基於使用
        on a value-in-use calculation. That calculation uses cash flow projections           價值計算確定的。該計算使用了管理層批准
        based on the business forecasts approved by management covering                      的業務預測，涵蓋五年的現金流量預測。超
        a period of five years. Cash flows beyond the five-year period are                   過五年期的現金流量使用穩定的年增長率進
        extrapolated using a steady annual growth rate. The pre-tax discount rate            行外推。二零二四年十二月三十一日使用的
        used in the value-in-use calculation as at 31 December 2024 was 13.3%.               稅前折現率為 13.3%。

        As the carrying amount of the Hong Kong CGU has been reduced to its                  由於香港現金產生單位的賬面金額已減少至
        recoverable amount, any adverse change in the assumptions used in the                其可收回金額，使用價值計算中假設的任何
        value-in-use calculation would result in further impairment losses.                  不利變化都會導致進一步的減值損失。

        The Group assessed the recoverable amount of the Hong Kong CGU and                   本集團已評估香港現金產生單元的可收回金
        concluded that no further impairment losses nor reversal of previously               額， 並 得 出 結 論： 截 至 二 零 二 五 年 十 二 月
        recognised impairment losses are required as at 31 December 2025.                    三十一日，無需進一步確認減值損失，亦無

24      Financial risk management and fair values                                       24    金融風險管理及公允價值
        Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks arises in the         本集團在一般業務過程中存在信貸、流動資
        normal course of the Group’s business. The Group’s exposure to these                  金、利率及外匯風險。本集團面對該等風險
        risks and the financial risk management policies and practices used by the            程度及本集團所採用管理該等風險之財務管
        Group to manage these risks are described below.                                      理政策及慣列，載述如下。

(a)     Credit risk                                                                     (a)   信貸風險
        Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its                信貸風險指交易對手違約其合約責任導致本
        contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the Group. The               集團財務虧損的風險。本集團的信貸風險主
        Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade receivables. The               要來自應收貿易賬項。本集團因現金及等同
        Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash and bank balances                   現金項目而產生的信貸風險有限，因為交易
        is limited because the counterparties are banks for which the Group                   對手為本集團認為信貸風險較低的銀行。
        considers to represent low credit risk.

        The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose the                      本集團並無因作出其他擔保而面對信貸風險。
        Group to credit risk.

        Trade receivables                                                                     應收貿易賬項

        The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by the individual            本集團的信貸風險主要受每名客戶的個別特
        characteristics of each customer rather than the industry or country in               徵影響，多於受客戶的經營所在地或行業的
        which the customers operate and therefore significant concentrations                  影響。因此，當本集團認為個別客戶有重大
        of credit risk primarily arise when the Group has significant exposure to             風險時，將會令信貸風險更為集中。於報告
        individual customers. At the end of the reporting period, 9% (2024: 11%)              期末，應收貿易及其他賬項總額的 9%（二零
        and 19% (2024: 24%) of the total trade and other receivables was due from             二 四 年：11%）及 19%（ 二 零 二 四 年：24%）
        the Group’s largest customer and the five largest customers respectively.             分別來自本集團最大客戶及五大客戶。

        The Group measures loss allowances for trade receivables at an amount                 本集團以終身預期信貸虧損的金額計量應收
        equal to lifetime ECLs, taken into account historical data, current                   貿易賬項的虧損撥備，並考慮到過往數據、
        conditions and the Group’s view of economic conditions over the                       現況及本集團對於在應收賬項預計年期內之
        expected lives of the receivables. Further quantitative disclosure in respect         經濟狀況的看法。本集團的應收貿易賬項產
        of the Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from trade receivables is set          生的信貸風險，其進一步的定量披露已載於

San Miguel Brewery Hong Kong Limited                                                致香港生力啤酒廠有限公司成員
(Incorporated in Hong Kong with limited liability)                                 （於香港註冊成立的有限公司）

OPINION                                                                            意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of San Miguel Brewery        本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於第 72
Hong Kong Limited (“the Company”) and its subsidiaries (“the Group”) set out       至 142 頁的香港生力啤酒廠有限公司（以下簡稱
on pages 72 to 142, which comprise the consolidated statement of financial         「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）
position as at 31 December 2025, the consolidated income statement,                的綜合財務報表，此財務報表包括於二零二五年
the consolidated statement of comprehensive income, the consolidated               十二月三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該日止
statement of changes in equity and the consolidated cash flow statement            年度的綜合收益表、綜合全面收益表、綜合權益
for the year then ended and notes, comprising material accounting policy           變動表和綜合現金流量表，以及附註，包括重大
information and other explanatory information.                                     會計政策資訊及其他解釋資訊。

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair view    我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師
of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31 December 2025         公會頒布的《香港財務報告會計準則》真實而中肯
and of its consolidated financial performance and its consolidated cash flows      地反映了貴集團於二零二五年十二月三十一日的
for the year then ended in accordance with HKFRS Accounting Standards as           綜合財務狀況及截至該日止年度的綜合財務表現
issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”)       及綜合現金流量，並已遵照香港《公司條例》妥為
and have been properly prepared in compliance with the Hong Kong                   擬備。
Companies Ordinance.


BASIS FOR OPINION                                                                  意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on                   我們已根據香港會計師公會頒布的《香港審計準
Auditing (“HKSAs”) as issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities under             則》進行審計。我們在該等準則下承擔的責任已
those standards are further described in the Auditor’s responsibilities for the    在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔的責
